## D-0154 · Selection is part of the randomness: holdout roles for regression, and rollbacks heal the arrays themselves

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The three findings of the eleventh review pass (STAT-RR11-1..3) are fixed at
  commit fdb655354102df26e2cef0964f8fad695917c302. Two are the same lesson stated for split
  conformal: the guarantee's randomness includes the CALIBRATION DRAW, so any decision that
  reads it -- promoting the candidate whose calibrated width shrank (expected coverage 0.90 ->
  0.7475 after twenty selections in the reviewer's exact construction), or deploying because
  qhat <= tol (conditional coverage 0.7513, not 0.90) -- is selection and voids the nominal
  statement. The regression solve loop now carries the classification loop's MXR-080-1891
  discipline: the holdout splits at solve time into immutable CALIBRATION rows (they set qhat,
  no promotion decision ever reads them) and SELECTION rows (they decide every improve()
  promotion and produce the reported measurements, with selection_uses recording when their
  numbers become selection scores); and the deployment-gate coverage claim is REMOVED at every
  site and replaced with the exact conditional counterexample plus a per-artifact MEASUREMENT
  (report()'s selection_coverage, computed on rows the quantile never touches). The third
  finding refines D-0152/D-0153's rollback rule at its last unprobed boundary: "restore in
  place, identity preserved" applies to the accumulator's OWN count arrays, not only pooled
  mappings and children -- a rollback that rebinds a pristine copy abandons the in-place-
  mutated original that a caller may alias. Count snapshots are now (original object, values)
  pairs and restoration writes INTO the original, in the shared helpers, so all thirteen
  accumulators heal at once.
- **Alternatives considered:** keeping promote-on-calibrated-width with a widened qhat
  (rejected: the bias grows with the number of candidates considered and is unbounded in
  principle; the role split removes the mechanism instead of taxing it); a PAC/training-
  conditional calibration mode to certify deployment-conditional coverage (deferred: new
  statistical machinery mid-cycle with its own delta bookkeeping; the review's required
  closure explicitly accepts reporting the deployment-conditional number as unguaranteed and
  measured, which the selection slice provides unbiasedly for a freshly solved artifact);
  hard-refusing selection-row reuse across improve() rounds (rejected: the no-argument
  improve() loop is the documented serving workflow -- reuse is recorded honestly via
  selection_uses, the classification precedent).
- **Evidence:** the reviewer's alias probe reproduced before the fix on the chain HMM and
  mixture with their exact values ([0.25, 0.75] aliased as [0.5, 1.5], identity lost) and
  healed after (values restored through the alias, identity preserved) on the scale, combine,
  and from_value paths; role-split behavior pinned by tests (promotions read selection rows
  only, roles never overlap, calibration rows survive improve() untouched, selection_coverage
  reports a single-use measurement); alias identity/value assertions ship for the chain/tree
  cores and across all eleven audited families; 275 tests + 397 subtests across the touched
  subsystems pass locally, including the keyed-protocol sweep and the duplicate-body gate.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (fixes directed in response to the eleventh external review pass, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit fdb655354102df26e2cef0964f8fad695917c302
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (pass eleven).

## D-0155 · A certifying slice must postdate everything it certifies

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The two findings of the twelfth review pass (STAT-RR12-1, STAT-RR12-2) are fixed
  at commit f7b5a44fc944bb6de3bd0de0e79d8e31b8f7cb5a. The rule that closes the whole
  selection-leakage family, stated once: split conformal's randomness includes the calibration
  draw, so a slice may certify only artifacts whose construction could not have depended on it
  -- and "construction" includes every upstream decision the slice influenced, not only direct
  comparisons. Pass eleven removed promote-on-qhat; pass twelve showed the remaining channel:
  the calibration-derived gate decides whether requests are harvested, the harvest builds the
  candidate, and the same slice then certified it (exact construction: 0.8857 against the
  claimed 0.90; fresh calibration restores 0.90). Regression improve() now calibrates every
  candidate on rows that postdate its harvest -- a reserved harvest tail (valid for regression
  because its all-or-none gate makes a closed route harvest the raw serving stream) or a
  teacher-labeled evidence_inputs batch -- and waits when the harvest cannot fund the slice.
  The classification loop's escalations are per-query threshold-selected, so they can serve
  neither role; its no-argument improve() keeps the documented workflow and now RECORDS the
  cost: both Solution classes carry a persisted calibration_evidence regime
  ("solve-split"/"fresh-harvest"/"fresh-evidence"/"reused-after-adaptive-harvest"), report()
  surfaces it, and the claim sites state that a reused-regime threshold is empirical. Every
  reported measurement carries its receipt: selection_coverage now travels with its denominator
  and an exact 95% Clopper-Pearson interval (STAT-RR12-2). The deterministic prelabeled=
  re-solve hazard is documented at the parameter: same inputs and seed reproduce the same
  calibration rows that gated the harvest, so a re-solved interval needs a fresh base sample.
- **Alternatives considered:** refusing no-argument classification promotions outright
  (rejected: the multilabel path's mandatory-fresh-evidence rule is right for its shape, but
  the classification serving loop is documented as self-contained and the anti-regression gate
  still protects agreement -- recording the voided regime is honest without breaking the
  workflow); reusing regression's escalated harvest without reservation on the grounds the gate
  is global (rejected: the reserved rows must also be excluded from candidate training, or they
  are construction data again); perturbing the deterministic split when prelabeled is supplied
  (rejected: cosmetic -- the leak is reusing the same base sample, not the same indices, and a
  shuffled split of the same rows certifies nothing more).
- **Evidence:** behavior pinned by tests: the reserved harvest tail becomes the artifact's
  calibration data and is disjoint from candidate training, improve() waits below the funding
  threshold, both ledger transitions fire on their promotion paths, and the coverage receipt's
  denominator and interval are asserted; 152 tests + 47 subtests across the task surface pass
  locally, plus the structured and multilabel suites. Also closed in the same commit: the
  pass-11 docstring-indentation Docs failure at a02d50d6, and a structured-output accounting
  test that assumed the pre-split holdout and had been executing in no CI job (the full shards
  lack torch, the optional shards select only optional-marked files) -- the coverage hole is
  flagged as follow-up work.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (fixes directed in response to the twelfth external review pass, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit f7b5a44fc944bb6de3bd0de0e79d8e31b8f7cb5a
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (pass twelve).

## D-0156 · CI must execute what it claims to cover, and say so with receipts

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The calibrated-serving test surface now executes in CI, at commit
  65310f8ad7cde025d4f4003167bfd395480d556a. The full-tier shards run without torch, so every
  torch-gated test class collected there and silently skipped, while the torch-installed
  optional shards select only torch-marked files -- twenty-four serving-surface files carried
  no such marker, so the suite ran in NO job. This was not hypothetical: a broken accounting
  identity in the structured-output tests stayed red from fdb65535 through a02d50d6 while CI
  showed green, and surfaced only in a local torch-enabled sweep. The fix applies the marker
  registry's own routing policy (backend-specific tests are optional-lane by construction):
  eighteen fully-torch-gated files join FILE_MARKERS with the torch marker, six registered
  files gain the marker they were missing, and three partially gated files route only their
  gated classes via class-level marks, keeping their torch-free tests in the full tier. Because
  selection is not execution, optional shard 1 additionally runs the four core serving files as
  a REQUIRED receipt through run_required_pytest.py, which fails on zero collected tests, any
  skip, failure, or error -- the surface cannot go silently dark again through marker, install,
  or selection drift. Separately, pip-audit began failing on PYSEC-2026-3624 (CVE-2026-58659,
  lightning <= 2.6.5 checkpoint-file remote code execution in load_from_checkpoint): no fixed
  release exists, and mixle's whole lightning surface is mini-batch data iteration and Fabric
  launch with no checkpoint loading anywhere, so the advisory enters the audited waiver file
  time-boxed to 2026-09-07 for re-review against a fixed release.
- **Alternatives considered:** installing torch in the full-tier shards (rejected: it re-times
  the budgeted tier around a heavyweight optional dependency and blurs the base-install
  correctness claim the full tier exists to make); wholesale re-tiering every torch-touching
  test file (~130 candidates; rejected for this change: most are partially gated, their
  torch-free tests belong in the full tier, and my automated classification is not reliable
  enough to move them safely -- the remaining partial-file torch classes are a smaller,
  enumerated residue for a follow-up); pinning lightning to a patched version (impossible:
  every 2.x release through 2.6.5 is affected and the fix commit is unreleased).
- **Evidence:** routing verified by collection: 64 tests across the moved files select under
  ``-m optional`` and zero under ``-m full``; the partial files split 3/5 as intended; the
  required receipt invocation executes 41 tests with zero skips locally; the waiver validates
  under both audit profiles (base emits no ignore arguments; all ignores exactly this
  advisory); 236 tests + 15 subtests across every touched and re-routed file pass locally.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (CI-coverage repair prompted by the pass-twelve audit trail, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit 65310f8ad7cde025d4f4003167bfd395480d556a
- **Expiration:** the PYSEC-2026-3624 waiver expires 2026-09-07; the routing and receipt are permanent.
- **Worklist:** CI coverage hole for torch-gated task suites (follow-up to external review pass twelve).

## D-0157 · A receipt that does not survive persistence is not a receipt

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The two findings of the thirteenth review pass (STAT-RR13-1, STAT-RR13-2) are
  fixed at commit cf34efb31b10f833fa1763cb4aff269c76ed8fa3. The honesty ledgers D-0155 introduced -- which regime
  certified the deployed threshold, and how many promotion decisions the selection rows have
  made -- were written into both artifact formats but never read back: a threshold saved as
  "reused-after-adaptive-harvest" (explicitly uncertified) reloaded as "solve-split"
  (certified), and selection_uses reset to zero, flipping the single-use flag back to True. The
  rule this pins: an honesty field is part of the artifact's identity, and every claim-bearing
  field written at save time must be restored -- with validation -- at load time, or the ledger
  is theater. Both loaders now restore the regime (refusing unrecognized values) and the
  selection count (refusing negatives), with round-trip pins in both suites. The same commit
  repairs the security workflow's SBOM passes to run under the accepted waiver arguments (the
  gating audit accepted the lightning waiver and the SBOM pass then failed on the same
  advisory), symmetrically in both profiles.
- **Alternatives considered:** defaulting an unrecognized stored regime to the reused
  (worst-case) label instead of refusing (rejected: silent coercion is how the last two ledger
  defects happened; the loader's validate-before-use discipline refuses what it cannot
  interpret); persisting the full selection_receipt list as well (deferred: the counts and
  regime are the claim-bearing fields; the per-round receipt list is diagnostic and its digest
  already binds the verification evidence).
- **Evidence:** round-trip tests in both solution shapes assert the regime, the count, the
  single-use flag, and report() after save/load; 82 tests across the touched task suites pass
  locally, and the loader refuses unrecognized regimes and negative counts by construction.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (fixes directed in response to the thirteenth external review pass, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit cf34efb31b10f833fa1763cb4aff269c76ed8fa3
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (pass thirteen).

## D-0158 · Remove the mechanism, not the instance

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The claim-bearing honesty ledger is now registry-driven, at commit
  5157e3728a2d108736a19eb01afd804ee88d379e. Three findings in this release were one defect: a claim field persisted by
  one hand-written path and restored -- or not -- by another (STAT-R1, STAT-RR13-1,
  STAT-RR13-2). Each was patched where it appeared while the mechanism survived; the release
  owner called this out, and the response is structural: each solution shape declares its
  ledger once (name, default, validator) in mixle/task/_ledger.py, save and load iterate the
  SAME declaration, restored values are validated with refusal rather than defaulting, and
  serialization validates on the way out so a corrupted live object cannot mint a well-formed
  artifact. The lifecycle contract suite iterates the registries too: a newly declared field
  fails the suite until round-trip sentinels exist, and the artifact tests distinguish the two
  defense layers (integrity digest refuses unsigned tampering; the ledger refuses validly
  signed but uninterpretable claims -- the suite's first draft passed vacuously on the wrong
  layer and was sharpened to name the required error). The same push repairs what the required
  serving lane's FIRST execution surfaced at b9a6234d: a real router escalation bug (per-item
  frontier teachers crashed on the first true escalation; now resolved through the shared
  TeacherCaller with a deterministic always-escalate regression), a platform-dependent
  device-budget test rewritten against its actual invariant, a one-platform quantize parity
  bound recalibrated with cross-platform headroom, and the two release contract tests that pin
  the waiver inventory and workflow shape, updated with the changes they exist to gate.
- **Alternatives considered:** continuing per-field persistence fixes as findings arrive
  (rejected: three instances of one mechanism is the definition of a structural defect);
  auto-deriving the ledger from dataclass field introspection (rejected: implicit persistence
  of everything is the opposite failure -- claim fields deserve declared defaults, validators,
  and sentinels); relaxing the corrupt-ledger response to a warning-and-default (rejected:
  silent defaulting is exactly how an uncertified threshold reloaded as certified).
- **Evidence:** the registry suite round-trips every declared field with non-default sentinels
  through both artifact formats, refuses corrupt values at write and read, verifies both
  defense layers by name, and pins receipt completeness (denominator, exact interval, reuse
  count, regime) on every report; 154 tests + 26 subtests across the batch surface pass
  locally, including the updated waiver and workflow-shape contracts, the router regression,
  and the recalibrated quantize proof.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (structural hardening directed after pass thirteen, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit 5157e3728a2d108736a19eb01afd804ee88d379e
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** calibration-code hardening (release-owner directive after external review pass thirteen).

## D-0159 · Absence of evidence is not evidence of a fresh solve

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The fourteenth review pass's finding (STAT-RR14-1) is fixed at commit
  3fc993ba2dd5d04461ea7028a3a72e8197d2c3f8: the honesty ledger is a REQUIRED member of the 0.8.0 artifact format, and
  an artifact missing any claim-bearing field -- or the verification block that carries them --
  is refused at load with re-solve guidance, never reinterpreted under fresh-solve defaults.
  The defect was subtle in exactly the way this ledger work keeps teaching: D-0157 established
  refusal-over-coercion for PRESENT-but-invalid values, and the registry then reused live-object
  initialization defaults as the meaning of ABSENT values -- so a validly signed legacy-shaped
  artifact whose threshold came from adaptive reuse, with selection evidence spent four times,
  reloaded as clean solve-split evidence with zero uses and a True single-use flag, and the
  lifecycle suite itself pinned that behavior as intended. Initialization defaults describe a
  freshly constructed object; an absent field describes an UNKNOWN history, and unknown history
  cannot present as certified. Refusal is chosen over an explicit unknown/non-certifying regime
  because 0.8.0 has never been published: no external pre-ledger artifact exists, an internal
  one is re-solved rather than reinterpreted, and an unknown-regime lane would be a permanent
  second-class certification path that itself needs auditing, maintained for a case that cannot
  legitimately occur.
- **Alternatives considered:** loading legacy-shaped artifacts into an explicit "unrecorded"
  regime with a conservative used-selection state (rejected as above: machinery for an
  impossible case, and every consumer of calibration_evidence would need to handle a fourth
  regime forever); refusing only the regime field while defaulting the count (rejected: the
  count drives the single-use honesty flag, which is exactly what flipped); treating the
  verification block's absence as merely non-promoted, as before (rejected: no block means no
  ledger, and the same unknown-history rule applies).
- **Evidence:** the lifecycle suite now asserts the required-ledger contract it previously
  contradicted: registry-level refusal of empty and per-field-partial metadata for both shapes,
  and the reviewer's exact construction -- validly SIGNED artifacts with each ledger field
  removed and the integrity digest recomputed -- refused by the ledger's own error for both
  formats, plus the no-verification-block case; 187 tests + 30 subtests across the
  artifact-loading surface pass locally with no fixture anywhere relying on ledger-less loads.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (fixes directed in response to the fourteenth external review pass, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit 3fc993ba2dd5d04461ea7028a3a72e8197d2c3f8
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (pass fourteen).

## D-0160 · Claims are inventory, not prose

- **Date:** 2026-08-07
- **Decision:** The fifteenth review pass's findings (STAT-RR15-1, STAT-RR15-2) are fixed at
  commit 26547c623e2bc97ab30910da8041e4b5aa862f9a, and the CLASS they belong to is closed mechanically at
  commit 07f6e28b44c29f80b8defda779fb03e7b4f8ac8f. The release owner named the root cause
  correctly: every claim-scope finding since pass nine is the same defect -- a free-text
  statistical claim somewhere in the package stronger than what the mathematics or the
  experiment establishes -- and it recurred because claims had no inventory, no gate, and no
  shared scope source; each was hand-written, drifted independently, and was found one external
  audit at a time. The closure is the package's own proven pattern (the Y4.5 duplicate-body
  manifest): a scanner enumerates every user-facing claim surface, a reviewed manifest
  inventories the 22 current sites, a ratchet contract test refuses new un-audited claim sites
  and inventoried sites whose scope disappears, and a shared conformal_scope constructor makes
  report-level scope machine-readable from one home. Freezing the inventory forced the
  whole-corpus audit the owner has been asking for: beyond the two named findings (the active
  example's fewer-calls claim reduced to its actual same-budget design with a paired interval
  and gated conclusion; joint-coverage scope stated at the structured and multilabel claim and
  report surfaces), seven more un-scoped coverage sites and five over-claiming comparative test
  docstrings were found and fixed in one sweep.
- **Alternatives considered:** continuing per-finding claim fixes (rejected: seven passes of
  evidence that the class regenerates faster than named-site fixes close it); a docs-only style
  guide (rejected: unenforced prose rules are how the corpus got here); gating on exact
  claim-text hashes (rejected: every edit would re-hash; the scope-token invariant is what
  matters and survives rewording); widening the gate to risk-certification vocabulary in
  mixle/reason's selective machinery (deferred: that language survived twelve passes because it
  is precise; the gate targets the class that actually produced findings, and its corpus can be
  widened by editing two regexes).
- **Evidence:** the scanner reports zero violations across 22 inventoried sites after the
  sweep, tuned to zero false positives on the current corpus (its first draft over-matched an
  error message and incidental prose, and under-matched the true claim lines -- both fixed
  before freezing); the rewritten active example runs end-to-end and its gated conclusion
  prints on measured evidence (paired difference +0.013, 95% CI [+0.000, +0.026] at the same
  budget); 183 tests + 70 subtests across the touched surfaces pass locally, including the new
  ratchet, the ledger lifecycle suite, and both release contract tests.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (root-cause closure directed after the fifteenth external review pass, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-07; commit 07f6e28b44c29f80b8defda779fb03e7b4f8ac8f
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (pass fifteen) and the claims-inventory root-cause directive.

## D-0161 · The answered slice is measured, not narrated

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision:** The sixteenth review pass's findings are fixed: STAT-RR16-1 at commit
  5254b9833fc91d51ad989f72b8929f60be29bd06 (both paired examples' conclusions now gate on the
  exact paired McNemar two-sided binomial tail over the discordant counts -- valid at any
  discordance count, where the Wald gate declared a difference at four one-sided discordances
  whose exact p-value is 0.125 -- with the Wald width kept only as a labeled descriptive
  companion, the conclusion rule importable and pinned by tests, and the active example's call
  ledger counting all 420 teacher calls including the 300 shared evaluation labels);
  STAT-RR16-2 at commit b7d13d8025e314dae733ccc127630d89ef28bd24 (every solution shape now
  MEASURES the answered slice by running its real serving rule over rows disjoint from every
  training and calibration row -- classification over its selection rows with the three counts
  riding CLASSIFICATION_LEDGER's refuse-on-missing registry, multilabel over its evaluation rows
  with required artifact members and a post_selection receipt flag on promotions, structured
  over a new record-level evaluation slice with the minimum input count raised to
  n_joint_cal + 14 -- all reporting the same answered_slice block with agreement, answered and
  evaluated denominators, and an exact 95% Clopper-Pearson interval, while the raw
  route-independent metrics are labeled raw at every surface; regression's all-or-none gate
  already had this in selection_coverage); STAT-RR16-3 at commit
  3adbc86878d4b523133e357577ec6828aee79317 (the claims gate's own description reduced to what it
  enforces, a COMPETITIVE class added requiring measurement-conditions disclosure, the
  COMPARATIVE and uncertainty vocabularies widened, negative and positive detector controls
  pinned, and the manifest regenerated at 27 reviewed sites). Commit
  353b6a4ca18ab2695c358b6e5345cfcdbd6ad56c additionally disclosed Cascade.plan()'s
  point-projection assumption at the API and example surfaces.
  This entry SUPERSEDES two statements in D-0160 under the append-only rule. First, D-0160 said
  the scanner "enumerates every user-facing claim surface"; it does not and cannot -- it is a
  lexical ratchet over three explicit phrase classes whose coverage grows by widening those
  classes, token presence cannot validate a statistical method, and the detector controls now
  pin exactly what each class does and does not match (this was STAT-RR16-3's finding). Second,
  D-0160's evidence quoted the active example's then-new conclusion gate approvingly ("paired
  difference +0.013, 95% CI [+0.000, +0.026]"); that gate was a Wald interval, invalid at the
  four observed discordances, and under the exact test that replaced it the same run is
  INCONCLUSIVE (p = 0.125) -- which is the honest verdict and is what the example now prints.
- **Alternatives considered:** prose-only relabeling of the raw metrics (rejected: the
  reviewer's minimal closure permitted it, but the package's premise is measured honesty, the
  mechanism already existed in regression's selection_coverage, and the measurement is cheap);
  measuring the structured answered slice on the joint calibration rows (rejected: the joint
  threshold is those rows' quantile, so the measurement would be optimistic by construction);
  defaulting absent answered-slice artifact members on load (rejected: STAT-RR14-1 -- a live
  object's zero-initialization means "not yet measured", and reusing it for an absent member
  would present an unknown measurement as one that measured nothing); a mid-p or asymptotic
  McNemar variant (rejected: the exact tail is conservative and simple, and these examples gate
  prose conclusions, not deployment decisions).
- **Evidence:** conclusion-rule tests pin rule(4,0) = 0.125, symmetry, the zero-discordance
  case, and a decisive case; both examples print the honest inconclusive branch on this machine
  (active 4-0 discordances p = 0.125; cascade 12-4 p = 0.0768); every shape's stored
  answered-slice counts are pinned equal to an independent rerun of its real serving rule, with
  save/load round-trip and missing-member refusal tests; the ledger lifecycle suite auto-covers
  the three new classification fields through its registry iteration; the claims gate passes at
  27 inventoried sites with detector negative/positive controls; the full local suite passes
  under the supported-range interpreter (15105 passed, 8691 subtests, 130 environment-gated
  skips, 0 failures); and all 57 examples run end-to-end against this tree
  (PYTHONPATH-pinned -- an unpinned first pass resolved the stale editable install instead and
  its 7 failures reproduced only there, so example evidence is valid only under the pin).
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-sixteen closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-08; commit b7d13d8025e314dae733ccc127630d89ef28bd24

## D-0162 · The drift alarm tests two samples, not a plug-in constant

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision:** Self-found between review passes while adversarially re-measuring the tree the
  way the external audit does. Solution.health() tested the live escalation count against the
  stored baseline escalation rate with a one-sample exact binomial test, treating an ESTIMATE
  from the selection rows as a known constant; measured under no drift at p_threshold = 0.01 it
  false-alarmed 61.6% / 56.6% / 71.1% of the time at (n_selection, n_live) = (10, 500) /
  (50, 1000) / (100, 5000), degrading as live traffic grows because baseline estimation error
  eventually dominates. Fixed at commit 3b039591dcad7061cd1eb0296f9f84ecf866f7ce: the escalation comparison is a two-sample exact
  test (Fisher) between the live counts and the selection-slice counts (0.4-0.8% measured on
  the same draws), with the baseline counts derived exactly from the answered-slice ledger
  (sel_rows - answered_sel_n), so loaded artifacts run the identical test and an object with no
  selection sample reports rates without a p-value. The OOD half was two-sided and alarmed on
  UNDER-firing -- the repo's own health test tripped it on same-world traffic (0.005 vs design
  0.05, p = 0.0009) over a point null that is false even under no drift (the design quantile is
  an in-sample threshold estimate); it is now one-sided greater, the novelty-flood direction the
  gate exists for.
- **Alternatives considered:** widening p_threshold while keeping the one-sample test (rejected:
  the size distortion grows without bound in live traffic, so no fixed threshold holds);
  Boschloo/Barnard unconditional tests (rejected: marginally more power than Fisher, materially
  more machinery; the alarm needs valid size, not optimal power); alarming on OOD under-firing
  too (rejected: not the hazard the gate exists for, and the null is false under H0 -- the rates
  remain reported for eyes).
- **Evidence:** the false-alarm simulation above (3000 replications per size pair); HealthTest
  now pins both directions (exchangeable traffic must NOT alarm and carries an escalation
  p-value; the shifted world still alarms); the wider re-measurement that surfaced this also
  Monte-Carlo-verified the shipped conformal paths -- classification LAC threshold exact at
  theory (mean qhat 0.89975 vs 18/20 over 50k order-statistic replications), multilabel joint
  coverage 0.9055 (nominal 0.90, ties conservative) with wrong-and-answered rate 0.0163 <=
  alpha, the shared rank rule at n = 9/19/49 within Monte-Carlo error of nominal, and
  solve_structured end-to-end joint record coverage 0.8135 (se 0.0303) at nominal 0.80 over 40
  full solves -- so the contract arithmetic itself measures clean.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (self-audit between passes sixteen and seventeen, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-08; commit 3b039591dcad7061cd1eb0296f9f84ecf866f7ce
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (standing hardening directive).

## D-0163 · The deferred items are closed, not deferred again

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision:** The two follow-ups this release had explicitly deferred are executed. First, the
  CI-dark torch residue D-0156 enumerated as "~100 partially-gated files, follow-up": measured
  precisely, it is 23 files whose torch-gated classes and methods executed in NO CI lane (full
  shards have no torch, so their gates skipped silently; the optional lane never selected the
  files). Every torch gate in them now carries a class- or method-level torch mark, which the
  conftest routing turns into optional-lane membership -- exactly 120 tests move, all execute
  locally (119 pass; 1 skips on a declared CUDA-hardware gate no hosted lane provides). Fixed at
  commit f4900770b76e4ea67a947f2793eda793dd4e0ec5. Second, the claims-gate widening D-0160 deferred ("that language survived twelve
  passes because it is precise"): precision is not enforcement, and measurement disagreed with
  the deferral -- a CERTIFICATION claim class (certify + risk/selective/accepted-error,
  selective-risk gate/threshold, (alpha, delta)-PAC, risk control) over task/reason/docs/example
  surfaces found seven claim sites of which one, PosteriorDescriber.calibrate in
  mixle/reason/language_bridge.py, advertised a certified selective-risk threshold while stating
  neither the sampling premise nor the failure probability. The gate now enforces the class
  (i.i.d./exchangeability premise + failure probability over the calibration draw + shift
  voiding, with ledger-provenance narration excluded by the verb-object claim shape and pinned
  by negative controls); language_bridge, CalibratedGenerator.calibrate, and the
  calibrated-report demo carry their scope statements. Fixed at commit 4683b5dd94a787e625ea5a9ee7d12c9a3766fc49; the manifest holds 32
  reviewed sites. This entry supersedes D-0156's residue note and D-0160's deferral sentence.
- **Alternatives considered:** file-level torch routing for the 23 files (rejected: it would
  evict their torch-free classes from the base-install lanes, shrinking coverage to grow it);
  leaving the certification vocabulary ungated another cycle (rejected: the measured gap in
  language_bridge is exactly the class the deferral assumed could not exist).
- **Evidence:** partition spot-check 40 optional + 14 full = 54 collected with nothing dropped;
  the 120 routed tests pass locally under the torch lane's own selector; claims gate green at 32
  sites with certification positive/negative controls; full local suite green (15105 passed,
  8691 subtests; one chaos-test failure under xdist load re-ran green single-process, the
  documented contention class).
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (deferred-item closure directed after pass sixteen, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-08; commit 4683b5dd94a787e625ea5a9ee7d12c9a3766fc49

## D-0164 · Core budget re-baselined to measured runner variance (fourth revision)

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision:** The core tier's 900-second budget was killed twice this week with every executed
  test green (once at 99% collected with all dots passing; a separate attempt died to a runner
  shutdown signal at 88%). Green-run core completions across the last four green Tests runs span
  300-404 seconds (n=20 jobs, both platforms), so the kills were runners pacing more than 2.2x
  the healthy range -- a budget at ~2.2x the worst green completion is a per-run coin flip
  against the degraded-runner tail, and a tripwire that fires on coin flips protects nothing.
  Core's budget is now 1500 seconds (~3.7x the worst green completion) with the job-level
  timeout backstop at 30 minutes, at commit e9604624e9ae4edf2f33acbbc16b15925e109e51; the tier doc and marker description, still
  reading "12 min" from two re-baselines ago, are synced to the enforced number.
- **Alternatives considered:** re-running failed lanes indefinitely (rejected: ~every other push
  has needed a manual re-run this week; that is operational noise, not protection); a smaller
  bump to 1200s (rejected: 1200 is 3.0x the worst green completion while the observed kill
  already implies >2.2x pacing, leaving one bad draw from another coin flip); moving core to
  larger runners (rejected for now: cost decision that belongs to the owner).
- **Evidence:** the duration measurements above (gh run durations across runs 31232420119,
  31177966791, 31175452949, 31165363097); the two budget/preemption kills on run 31239457632
  attempts 1-2 with zero failing tests; tier and smoke contract suites green after the change.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (CI-noise root-cause after two same-week budget kills, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-08; commit e9604624e9ae4edf2f33acbbc16b15925e109e51
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** CI reliability (D-0144 lineage).

## D-0165 · The inference surface gets the same audit the task surface got

- **Date:** 2026-08-08
- **Decision:** Sixteen external passes never entered mixle/inference; a user-directed sweep of
  its 17 claim-bearing modules (three parallel read-only audits over five defect classes, plus
  in-session reproduction of the sharpest items) found the surface materially defective, and the
  campaign now runs off the tracked ledger
  release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md under the standing rules: reproduce
  before fixing, one fix per finding with adverse tests in the same commit (D-0147), no
  fixed/outstanding ratios without per-finding reproduction. First wave, all reproduced then
  fixed: mechanical in-sample receipts and the ungated coverage-claim surface at commit 7d62f56746102d822ed29642f96b0661dfe433aa
  (uq.py relabel; claims gate extended to mixle/inference with negated-mention suppression;
  score-anchor and sum-vs-mean docstring falsehoods); forecast() joint sample paths at commit
  a03c4f2d8865b487ba616b1ce3c9a1d313649c71 (the old draws fed Monte-Carlo NPV percentiles with no true serial dependence and a
  state-sorted ordering artifact -- 0.69 measured against a -0.03 shuffle control -- and the
  adverse test now pins the empirical lag-1 correlation to the exact analytic joint value,
  which at a near-pure start is ~0.70, not the stationary 0.96 the first draft of the test
  wrongly assumed); exact small-sample nulls in Wilcoxon and two-sample KS at commit 2978cf065152c0f3d829c8ff9f848aa19cadcf3e (the
  normal branch REJECTED an n=5 outcome whose exact p is 0.0625 -- a guaranteed 6.25% type-I
  rate at nominal 5% -- and ks_2samp returned p=0.0 where the exact answer is 0.1); the
  m-out-of-n pivotal orientation and the m==n refusal at commit 67bddd7a95abdbb945391b1800e4f6d193d5a1b7 (percentile orientation gave
  exactly zero coverage on the canonical sample-max example, measured 0 -> 1.00 conservative
  after the flip; m==n returned a zero-width interval). E-1 (event_study Poisson effects:
  p=6.2e-36 under a TRUE NULL with unequal exposures at 1000 subjects, reproduced in-session)
  is recorded as design-scale: the per-subject Gaussianization is structurally invalid at
  sparse counts, and the repair -- an information floor plus an exact conditional pooled route
  -- lands in the next wave after bias-vs-count measurement, not as a tail-of-session patch.
- **Alternatives considered:** treating the agent reports as findings without reproduction
  (rejected: the audit's own first test draft mis-specified the forecast benchmark, which the
  reproduce-first rule caught); fixing E-1 with a quick per-subject count floor chosen by eye
  (rejected: guard-overreach discipline -- the floor must come from measured bias curves);
  leaving the sweep results in session notes (rejected: the worklist pattern is how every
  prior wave in this release stayed auditable).
- **Evidence:** each fix's commit carries its measured before/after numbers and adverse tests;
  the ledger records the judged-acceptable list so the non-findings are auditable too; the
  full local suite and CI on the pushed tip gate the wave as with every batch this release.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (inference-surface audit directed after pass sixteen, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-08; commit a03c4f2d8865b487ba616b1ce3c9a1d313649c71
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md (open items).


## D-0166 · Passes seventeen and eighteen are closed at the finding level

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision:** Every finding from external passes seventeen (20) and eighteen (15 High) carries
  a fix with adverse verification in the same commit. The decisive closures: the calibration
  gate was rebuilt twice and is now an exact column-swap randomization test with ONE invariant
  jitter across relabelings (my level simulation at the reviewer's own k=10/m=4/alpha=.21
  regime: 0.1303 against their 0.1298 same-jitter control) and power that EXECUTES the decision
  under both dependence regimes, gated on the caller-declared regime (a17f95de80da6e6f4f461cb0c51d5797ba84e518); the risk bound
  counts the declared (prompt, outcome) unit, closing the certify-from-duplicates
  contradiction (efe21493e77d44287290c4c0ba2d8fd872545e9a); propose() generates candidates from training rows only (32d6f2b4ba0408b99e9f8acbe1c84972a25f2d50); sparse
  Poisson Gaussianization refuses below a MEASURED floor (bias/SE 0.48 at expected count 0.1
  down to 0.004 at 4) with an exact conditional pooled route in its place, level-verified at
  0.041/0.05 over 2000 replicates of the reviewer's null (b84de41bcc000e372b1e57dc4988a43d407d829a); and the example suite now earns
  its printed claims -- all-in call ledgers (1,020; $6.563 vs $3.00 with the costs-more
  admission), genuinely reused harvests (690 calls where the old flow spent 1,312),
  per-stratum exact inference under the stratified design, and recovery oracles that
  structurally fail wrong category identities and no-op fits. A recurring resolution pattern
  this pass: where validity depends on a premise only the caller can know (ensemble
  construction, outcome attachment, model-fit boundary), the API now takes the DECLARATION,
  records it on the receipt, and gates its guarantees on the declared regime -- fail-safe
  defaults, never silent assumptions.
- **Alternatives considered:** shipping the pass-17 gate fix after only its own adverse tests
  (rejected by events: pass 18 measured it invalid at regimes my tests could not reach -- the
  standing protocol is now a self-run level simulation at attainable-alpha regimes and under
  every claimed dependence regime before any decision rule lands); treating reviewer-confirmed
  ledger items as closable by annotation (rejected: E14.2 counts open findings, not diagnoses).
- **Evidence:** per-commit measured receipts (level simulations, exhaustive enumerations,
  bias-curve measurements, before/after example outputs); the full local suite on the final
  tree; CI on the pushed tip.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-seventeen/eighteen closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-09; commit a17f95de80da6e6f4f461cb0c51d5797ba84e518
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** external statistical review (passes seventeen and eighteen); release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md carries the remaining self-found open items.

## D-0167 · The audit's own backlog is emptied, not curated

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision:** Every open item in the self-audit ledger's "Demonstrated" backlog is fixed --
  none re-triaged, none re-deferred -- one commit per module cluster, each carrying measured
  receipts: proper-by-default ensemble scores (the improper 1/m^2 form's dispersion minimizer
  measured 0.700 of truth), ddof-1 epistemic variance and a log(M)-capped BALD MI, opt-in GPD
  tail extrapolation (fitted-tail q99.9 sd 46.4 against 2.36 empirical at n=100), Student-t GLM
  references when dispersion was estimated (level 9% -> 5.2% at n=8) with rank-deficiency
  refusal of per-coefficient Wald, grouped-jackknife frailty SEs (se/SD 0.84 -> 0.98), the
  ADVI alpha-near-1 tolerance band (round-off measured rising to 2.3e-3 at 1-alpha=1e-13) with
  the K-sample objective disclosed as K-dependent, finite-sample calibration nulls
  (calibration_null_expectation; coverage_curve null_expectation; MCE null 0.057 -> 0.85 as
  bins grow), exact-tie Page variance (tied-null level 5.0% measured), the Brunner-Munzel
  separation bound, Fisher-routed small Mood tables, circular moving blocks with a mid-p BCa
  z0 and a labelled degenerate-jackknife fallback, Davidson-Flachaire leverage in the wild
  bootstrap (slope CI 0.36 -> 0.50 on a high-leverage design), the winner's curse surfaced on
  bayes_action (reproduced at -2.52 MC SEs, repaired to +0.08 by opt-in fresh-draw reporting
  without adding a single default-path loss call), the alpha-decided N=2 selection flag
  declared undefined, and per-lead-marginal price-forecast receipts. Two overclaims found
  while fixing were corrected in the same wave rather than banked: the calibration gate's
  "distribution-free by ranks" benchmark wording (linear interpolation leaves a measured
  0.632/0.644/0.636 family spread at m=5) and a stale test pin still encoding the
  pre-RR17-02 Haldane-at-zero contract.
- **Alternatives considered:** fixing only reviewer-confirmed items and leaving the
  self-demonstrated ones as documentation debt (rejected: the pass-18 lesson is that every
  demonstrated number eventually becomes an external finding); split-half winner reporting as
  the bayes_action default (rejected: it would break the exact-invocation-count guarantee
  MXR-080-1611 that protects metered losses -- disclosure default, unbiased opt-in).
- **Evidence:** per-cluster commits bda80a3c..f9ca5ef0 with in-commit measured receipts and
  adverse tests; the audit ledger's closure-wave section records every number; the full local
  suite on the final tree and CI on the pushed tip gate the batch.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (own-ledger closure wave, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-09; commit 6a9f1c64381a65289f554630e4c0d2839c8779fd
- **Expiration:** none.

## D-0168 · Pass nineteen's named blockers die by reproduction, not description

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision:** The four blockers named in the pass-nineteen delivery are closed with in-session
  reproductions on both sides of each fix. The Poisson batch route's failure was NOT the floor
  it was gated on: count-estimated inverse-variance weights anti-correlated with the effects
  (corr -0.72) and rejected 49-100% of true nulls FLAT in n -- arm-level exact plug-in variances
  make the weights inert by construction and exact pmf-summation debiasing leaves a residual
  that shrinks with n (measured 0.060/0.062/0.040 at n=1e3/1e4/1e5). The duplicate collapse was
  certifying the WRONG estimand, not a wrong number: uniform-over-distinct-prompts while serving
  experiences traffic weight (certified 0.080 vs served 0.373-0.410, deterministic); the repair
  is the house declaration pattern's third axis -- sampling='constructed'|'iid-traffic' -- with
  the certified estimand NAMED on every receipt and the iid-traffic declaration refusing the
  blocker stream outright. The tolerance modes promoted on the p-value test's power, a test they
  never ran; they now execute the caller's own rule and disclose its measured null level. And
  the MCMC summary judged chain count from the pooled draw shape, making 'ok' unreachable for
  every real multi-chain fit while asserting 'one chain' beside a finite multi-chain R-hat;
  chain count now comes from the fit's own convergence evidence, and the single-chain NaN
  markers are no longer mislabeled numeric failures. semantic_entropy_receipt no longer reports
  n_samples=0 with an uncorrected value for iterator inputs.
- **Alternatives considered:** waiting for the lost report before fixing anything (rejected: the
  four named blockers were independently reproducible and each is a serving-risk or false-
  rejection class; the report re-drop gates only the REMAINING itemized findings); repairing the
  Poisson route by raising the count floor again (rejected by measurement: the weighting channel
  is flat in n and no floor touches it).
- **Evidence:** in-session reproductions and post-fix level/estimand measurements recorded in
  the ledger's pass-19 section and in each commit (c3df7f58, ed8d18e4, fbaec7ce, 97bcaf2d); the
  full local suite on the final tree and CI on the pushed tip gate the batch, as always.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-nineteen named-blocker closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-09; commit 4b057aa7b0d6747e4dfe23e6788d9f792cdc8170
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md (pass-19 section; remaining itemized findings blocked on the report re-drop).

## D-0169 · Pass twenty closes by conditioning, bounding, and aligning -- not by patching

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision:** All twenty pass-twenty findings are fixed with reproduce-first evidence. The
  three structural closures: the Poisson batch route moves to the CONDITIONAL likelihood --
  given each subject's total, the baseline rate cancels exactly, which is the only way the
  heterogeneous-subject estimand the route always claimed becomes reachable (the reviewer's
  half-0.1/half-9.1 null: 400/400 rejections before, 0.055/0.0417 after, power 1.000 retained);
  gate promotion now compares exact one-sided 90% confidence BOUNDS with its floors -- power
  LCB >= 0.5, and for caller tolerances additionally null-rejection UCB <= 0.5 and below the
  power LCB, which kills both the 0.55-point-estimate promotion of a 0.467-power gate and the
  tolerance whose 'passed' was 3.18x likelier under miscalibration; and the semantic-entropy
  gate calibrates and serves ONE statistic (Miller-Madow at a recorded n, enforced at serving),
  ending the 98%-vs-41% acceptance split between two estimators of the same name. The
  swap test's exactness claim is SCOPED to its two invariance regimes after reproducing the
  83.4% false alarm on shared-latent rows with fresh entries -- 'independent' now declares row
  independence too, and the shared-draw construction is named as the universal remedy. Wilcoxon
  and runs-test exact ceilings turned out to be historical, not computational, and were raised
  (25 -> 300; 60 -> 5000) after measuring the tails they starved. The Brunner-Munzel separation
  p-value is honestly NaN with the exchangeability bound labeled as exactly that. Example
  economics are paired-and-gated or odometer-complete; the remaining mediums are assignment
  guards, floors, vocabulary alignment, and receipts saying precisely which count the bound
  uses.
- **Alternatives considered:** raising the Poisson count floor again (rejected by measurement:
  the failure is estimand-level and flat in the floor); keeping the separation p-value under a
  bigger disclaimer (rejected: a p-value invalid under the test's own null is not a disclaimer
  problem); widening the swap test to a third regime (rejected: no invariance exists there --
  the claim shrinks instead, per E14.2's fix-or-reduce).
- **Evidence:** per-cluster commits b37ca54c..2b1db534 with in-commit measurements; the ledger's
  pass-20 section records every number; the report and Q6 artifact are archived under
  ~/mixle/review-archive/ after the pass-19 report was destroyed unread by a disk-full cleanup.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-twenty closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-09; commit ece49f3e6c7b3d09a3a8e01da0f2f6262573820a
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md (pass-20 section).

## D-0170 · Pass twenty-one: the guarantees now name their policies, weights, and chains

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Decision:** All seventeen pass-twenty-one findings are fixed, reproduce-first. The pattern
  across the six Highs is one lesson stated three ways: a guarantee is a statement about a
  SPECIFIC object -- an estimand, a policy, a chain structure -- and every failure came from
  computing something adjacent and letting the name carry over. The identified event-study path
  now estimates the arithmetic ATT it names (equal-subject weights; the DL contrast rejected a
  true zero ATT 92-100% because precision weights track effect-dependent totals); the LLM
  selective-risk certificate names its generator, equivalence, and sample count and refuses to
  serve any other policy (an n-override had served 60.15% risk under a 10% certificate);
  ensemble diagnostics treat walkers as the chains they are (flattening them understated MCSE
  3.97x); cross-modal conformal splits scale estimation from ranking so the query's score is
  exchangeable with what gets ranked (0.179 joint coverage at d=50 before; 0.899-0.905 finite-
  box coverage after); weighted conformal takes each query's own likelihood ratio (the shared
  default covered 0.589); and the active-label example checks direction before declaring a
  winner. The mediums make the receipts carry what the decisions used (power LCBs, null UCBs,
  declared regimes), the thresholds exact (order-statistic q95), the guards transactional, the
  SEs non-degenerate (bootstrap over the delta method), and the exact tails representable
  (big-integer sums with log10 magnitudes).
- **Alternatives considered:** keeping DL weighting under a renamed estimand for the identified
  path (rejected: the receipt-bearing label IS "ATT", and the caller attaching an identification
  receipt is asking for the treated-population mean, not a precision-weighted functional);
  binding the LLM certificate by warning instead of refusal (rejected: RR17-07 set the
  precedent -- a certificate that survives policy changes is the certify-low-serve-high
  mechanism with extra steps).
- **Evidence:** per-cluster commits 176a45a1..ab4eedd9, each carrying its reproduction and
  post-fix measurements; the ledger's pass-21 section records every number; reports archived
  under ~/mixle/review-archive/.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-twenty-one closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-09; commit bcb1b22bf36a3fbfcad4dcfeb275d1d0d20b7d89
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md (pass-21 section).

## D-0171 · Pass twenty-two: selection is part of the estimand, behavior is part of the policy

- **Date:** 2026-08-10
- **Decision:** All seventeen pass-twenty-two findings are fixed, reproduce-first. The Highs
  divide into two families. ESTIMAND INTEGRITY: the conditional Poisson route's zero-total
  exclusion is outcome-dependent, so its receipt now travels with the effects and the
  identified result names the event-positive population it actually estimates (a true-zero
  all-treated ATT had read -0.94 with 100% rejection); the identified path's uncertainty floors
  its empirical variance at the supplied sampling contribution and takes p and CI from one
  Student-t reference (the SE-0/CI-[1,1]/p-1 self-contradiction is impossible by construction
  now); the backwards-KL example and the memorization example each print the quantity they
  compute, gated where a claim needs earning. STATE BINDING: refitting clears cross-modal
  conformal radii; the two sibling conformal surfaces state the behavioral-immutability
  condition their arithmetic always assumed; the LLM certificate documents what identity
  refusals cannot catch and records a caller-pinned policy token. And the ensemble sampler's
  walkers now start from prior draws with R-hat over ensembles and an 'ok' that enforces the
  standard thresholds -- the one-mode certification (R-hat 1.0095, ok, 4,054 MCSEs of error)
  cannot recur at any tested seed.
- **Alternatives considered:** dropping zero-total subjects silently but widening the SE
  (rejected: no variance inflation renames a population); behavioral fingerprinting of
  predictors via probe batches (rejected for now: per-serve probe costs and false-positive
  churn against honest in-place no-ops; the conditioning language plus cleared-on-refit state
  covers the library's own mutation paths, and the policy token gives infrastructure the
  version hook); walker-level R-hat with a bimodality heuristic (rejected: interacting walkers
  are not independent evidence at any threshold -- the unit of replication is the ensemble).
- **Evidence:** commits 7c95f0f4..bfb70fa5 with in-commit reproductions of every reviewer
  number; the ledger's pass-22 section records them; reports archived under
  ~/mixle/review-archive/. Every pass-21 closure held on the reviewer's own exact replay.
- **Owner:** release owner.

## D-0172 · Pass twenty-three: no label outlives its estimator, no promotion outlives its pathology

- **Date:** 2026-08-10
- **Decision:** All twelve pass-twenty-three findings are fixed, reproduce-first. The deepest
  reversal is self-inflicted and instructive: pass twenty-two's "rename the population" repair
  for the Poisson route was itself falsified -- the debiasing cannot hit even the renamed
  event-positive target under heterogeneous effects (bias -0.092, 0/80 coverage) -- so the
  honest end state is the one the reviewer offered from the start: outcome-selected contrasts
  are ASSOCIATIONS, whatever receipts accompany them, and the ATT label survives only drop-free
  data. Elsewhere the wave completes fail-closed symmetry: CrossModal fitting is transactional
  (validate before mutate, invalidate before mutate); factuality verdicts get the same
  exact-Boolean guard its siblings earned two passes ago; walker provenance is stamped at the
  sampler so no execution path can manufacture convergence the sequential path refuses; and
  recorded NUTS divergences block the only-promotable status. The Welch route now states the
  distributional condition finite-sample t inference always had, with the measured 31%
  skew-law failure attached to every identified result.
- **Alternatives considered:** estimating the event-positive mean with per-subject debiasing
  (rejected: the per-subject corrections are noise-dominated at exactly the counts where
  exclusion bites -- the association reduction is the only claim the estimator supports);
  enforcing a skewness gate on the Welch route (rejected: a data-driven normality screen at
  n=5 is itself unreliable -- the condition is stated, measured, and priced instead);
  probabilistic divergence tolerance (rejected: a recorded divergence is direct evidence of
  unexplored geometry -- there is no honest nonzero threshold).
- **Evidence:** commits 2e24bfe6..403b2dd6 plus regression pins; the ledger's pass-23 section
  records every number; reports archived under ~/mixle/review-archive/. Twelve of seventeen
  pass-22 closures held fully on the reviewer's exact replay.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (pass-twenty-three closure, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-10; commit 5fb5f2095c8be520877cd67a09dab09aa11c1c51
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** release-checklists/inference-stats-audit-2026-08-08.md (pass-23 section).

## D-0174 · Re-running the notebook corpus against the current tip surfaces and fixes a real Spark-backend crash

- **Date:** 2026-08-10
- **Decision:** Re-executing `mixle-notebooks` against the current `release/0.8.0` tip (not the
  2026-08-06 candidate the existing manifest measured) found one genuine regression:
  `tutorials/estimation_using_spark.ipynb` crashed inside `optimize()` over a Spark RDD with
  `ValueError: Markov statistics have no declared or observed states`. Root cause:
  `MarkovChainAccumulator.value()` hard-refused whenever it held no data, a guard meant to catch a
  misconfigured estimator. Spark's shuffle-merge legitimately calls `.value()` on a per-partition
  accumulator that saw zero rows for that mixture component -- an empty accumulator there is a
  transient, correct state, not a caller error, and the same call path is reachable through
  `MixtureAccumulator.from_value`'s pre-mutation transactional snapshot on any backend. Fixed by
  returning an empty statistics tuple instead of raising, and teaching `combine()`/`from_value()`
  to treat an empty incoming tuple as an identity element rather than routing it through the
  strict validator built for user-facing calls. The guard's real purpose is unaffected: a caller
  that actually tries to `estimate()` a distribution from genuinely empty statistics is still
  refused, with better context, by the existing `_validate_markov_statistics` check at that call
  site -- verified directly, alongside the fixed accumulator round-tripping empty and real data
  correctly in either merge order.
- **Alternatives considered:** working around it in the notebook (rejected: the notebook does
  nothing unusual -- `optimize()` over an RDD is exactly mixle's documented Spark contract, so the
  bug is the library's); catching and swallowing the exception at the Spark merge call site
  (rejected: that would hide the same defect from every other caller of `snapshot_accumulator_statistics`
  and `from_value`, not just this one).
- **Evidence:** `mixle/stats/sequences/markov_chain.py` (`MarkovChainAccumulator.value`/`combine`/
  `from_value`); regression test `markov_chain_contracts_test.py::test_empty_accumulator_value_combines_and_reconstructs_without_raising`;
  collateral batch across markov_chain/mixture/effective-sample/transaction tests, 98 passed;
  notebook re-executed clean end to end (105.7s) after the fix, against the worktree tip.
- **Owner:** release owner.
- **Reviewer:** accepted — Grant Boquet, release owner (found and fixed during release-readiness notebook re-evaluation, implementation AI-verified); 2026-08-10; commit a5dd09d9df1175db655d478c29aab8805d48699c
- **Expiration:** none.
- **Worklist:** none (found outside the statistical-review campaign, during notebook re-validation).
